Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the optimization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 214. The moduli space has dimension at most 32 with 5 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the optimization space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 214 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 32. QED.
